DA16/M22 joint executed follow-up: on the identical 9-variable affine plane with coordinate IDs 3i+j, all three pairings of its four line partitions, and root / actual x_0=0 / x_0=1 separately, each individual side has 102 (root) or 51 (pinned) models, complete ambient affine hull, and no degree-2 or degree-3 BPC rejection.
